# Skills · L · part 21

788 entries, lcms-processing-software-benchmarking to lead-gen. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **lcms-processing-software-benchmarking**: Use when comparing the computational efficiency (CPU time, memory, scaling with sample number) of LC-MS metabolomics processing tools on centroided mzML datasets, or when validating claims that a new tool scales better than established ones like XCMS. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-processing-software-benchmarking))
- **lcms-qc-normalization-benchmarking**: Use when comparing the computational cost of QC sample-based normalization methods on targeted (e.g., 53-compound) vs untargeted (e.g., 8913-feature) metabolomics matrices, or when deciding which normalization method is feasible for high-throughput use. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-qc-normalization-benchmarking))
- **lcms-quality-metric-interpretation**: Use when targeted LC–MS peak integration output must be quality-filtered: peaks carry computed metrics (AUC, max intensity, POP, SNR, bell-curve similarity) and 'Good'/'Bad'/'Ambiguous' calls must be evaluated or reproduced. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-quality-metric-interpretation))
- **lcms-raw-data-autotuning-benchmark**: Use when raw LC-MS metabolomics data must be processed and you need to decide which auto-tuning method (Paramounter, AutoTuner, or IPO) yields the most true positive features before committing to a peak-picking parameter set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-raw-data-autotuning-benchmark))
- **lcms-raw-data-import**: Use when raw vendor or open-format LC-MS files need to be loaded into MZmine 2 before peak picking, or when designing a processing pipeline that must import MS data without precision loss from resampling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-raw-data-import))
- **lcms-raw-data-import**: Use when you have raw LC/MS data in mzML or mzXML format and need to initiate untargeted metabolomics analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lcms-raw-data-import) · 2 copies)
- **lcms-raw-data-parameter-measurement**: Use when starting an untargeted LC-MS metabolomics workflow with raw spectra and you need data-driven values for mass tolerance, peak height, peak width, or instrumental shift instead of relying on vendor defaults or manual tuning. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-raw-data-parameter-measurement))
- **lcms-raw-data-reading-mzml**: Use when you have raw LC-MS mzML files (e.g., pooled/intrastudy QC, blanks, and study plasma samples) that must be ingested by TidyMS to extract spectral features for a quality-control or data-curation pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-raw-data-reading-mzml))
- **lcms-raw-file-format-conversion**: Use when raw LC-MS/MS files (e.g. Orbitrap .raw) must be converted to mzXML so the GlyKAn AZ app's MS1 Peak ID / MS2 Peak Confirmation tabs can ingest them. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-raw-file-format-conversion))
- **lcms-raw-spectra-preprocessing-to-npy**: Use when starting from raw LC-MS metabolomics files (e.g. mzML from MetaboLights studies such as MTBLS1129) that must be auto-converted to .npy arrays for end-to-end deep-learning classification with DeepMSProfiler. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-raw-spectra-preprocessing-to-npy))
- **lcms-replicate-retention-time-analysis**: Use when you have three or more replicate mzXML/mzML injections of a chemical-standard mixture (e.g. from comparing HILIC LC-MS mobile phases) and need per-peak quality metrics, validated peaks, and replicate-consistent retention times. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-replicate-retention-time-analysis))
- **lcms-result-table-structure**: Use when after completing Part 4 (Identification of ISF Features) in the ISFrag workflow, when you have an analysis results object containing identified ISF features and need to generate a shareable, tabular export that documents feature annotations, hierarchical parent–fragment relationships, and. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lcms-result-table-structure) · 2 copies)
- **lcms-retention-time-alignment**: Use when when XCMS-aligned LC-MS data shows coefficient of variation (CV) above expected thresholds for known features, or when analyzing long-duration experiments (&gt;1 week) or large cohorts (&gt;100 samples) where global warping functions are known to fail due to compound-specific RT drift structures. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lcms-retention-time-alignment) · 2 copies)
- **lcms-retention-time-bounding-box-matching**: Use when you have full-scan mzML peak-picked bounding boxes (min/max RT and m/z) plus a TopN mzML schedule and need the upper bound on fragmentable peaks for a single injection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-retention-time-bounding-box-matching))
- **lcms-scan-timing-analysis**: Use when comparing DDA acquisition controllers' scan overhead — e.g. mzML files from full-scan, TopN, SmartROI, and WeightedDEW runs need inter-scan start-time deltas converted into MS1 scan-plus-processing timings to verify controller processing overhead. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-scan-timing-analysis))
- **lcms-signal-patch-partitioning**: Use when raw LC/MS data in mzML format must be converted to an MS1 ion-feature table (median rt, m/z, normalized intensities) and no QC samples or user-tuned peak-detection thresholds are available. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-signal-patch-partitioning))
- **lcms-spectral-metadata-serialization**: Use when a curated PeakForest compound/spectrum collection (JSON/CSV peak lists with chromatography and ionization metadata) must be exported for MassBank/MoNA submission or consumed by tools like MS-DIAL and NIST MSsearch. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-spectral-metadata-serialization))
- **lcms-spectral-peak-classification**: Use when you have raw LC-MS spectral peak data (in the format provided by DOI 10.25345/C5FD2F) and need to build a classifier that can distinguish valid peaks from false positives or background noise without manual feature engineering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lcms-spectral-peak-classification) · 2 copies)
- **lcms-standard-blank-intensity-ratio-filtering**: Use when building or validating a retention-time reference library from chemical standard mixtures measured in triplicate LC-MS runs and peaks must pass blank-vs-standard (Std/Blk), RSD, and intensity thresholds before recording. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-standard-blank-intensity-ratio-filtering))
- **lcms-structural-cluster-linking**: Use when you have statistically significant features from untargeted multi-assay LC-MS metabolomics datasets and want to group them into structural clusters based on retention time and mass-to-charge ratio, enabling putative annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-structural-cluster-linking))
- **lcms-system-suitability-assessment**: Use when evaluating UHPLC-HRMS acquisition performance prior to data collection — e.g., assessing RT stability, peak areas, or MS/MS product ion consistency of an SST standard mixture acquired over months by multiple operators. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-system-suitability-assessment--dea3c698a9c7dcef))
- **lcms-system-suitability-assessment**: Use when raw LC–MS mzML files from a plasma reference material batch (e.g., NIST candidate RM 8231 or SRM 1950, MTBLS1919 deposit) must be checked for system suitability using the five SSS standard compounds and pooled/intrastudy QC injections. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-system-suitability-assessment--c2ab6faab7c65d9c))

Int., SNR, bell-curve similarity, points over the peak) for large cohorts. - **lcms-workflow-orchestration**: Use when starting from raw LC-MS spectral files (mzML or mzXML format) in a global metabolomics study and you need to produce a complete, validated feature table with m/z, retention time, and intensity values across all samples. - **lcmsms-fragmentation-image-encoding**: Use when you have MS-DIAL-preprocessed LC-MS/MS feature tables with precursor (MS1) and fragment (MS2) m/z pairs plus intensities and need fixed-size image inputs for CNN-based bioactivity prediction of complex metabolomics extracts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcmsms-fragmentation-image-encoding))

Use when setting up or rebuilding the investigation database, verifying corpus completeness against the data manual, or fetching a raw record from a citation key. ([comp-journalism/aaijc-workflows-release](https://github.com/comp-journalism/aaijc-workflows-release/tree/HEAD/submissions/data-folks/skills/lda-corpus-loader) · ★11 · 2 copies)
- **lda-corpus-toolkit**: Deterministic tooling for investigating US federal lobbying disclosures (Senate LDA JSON, House LDA XML, LD-203 contributions) plus congressional press releases. Indexes the corpus into SQLite, joins the two chambers exactly, dedupes amendment restatements, and ships analysis instruments (cross-chamber discrepancy classifier, bridge-key audit, revolving-door miner, LD-203 non-filer detector, fee rate-card outliers, spend-vs-messaging quiet zones). Use when working with LDA/LD-203 filings or lobbying-influence questions. ([comp-journalism/aaijc-workflows-release](https://github.com/comp-journalism/aaijc-workflows-release/tree/HEAD/submissions/martin-with-claude/skills/lda-corpus-toolkit) · ★11)
- **lda-detect**: Anomaly + topic detectors over an indexed LDA corpus — lobbying-spend restatements, House-vs-Senate mismatches, disclosure-after-the-fact (back-filing), a topic influence scan with per-entity anomaly flags, and a say-vs-pay join. Every detector emits ranked candidate lists, never findings; scope them to a topic and mine the whole list via a subagent (`scan_topic --shortlist`, never a full Read), then verify each with lda-verify. ([comp-journalism/aaijc-workflows-release](https://github.com/comp-journalism/aaijc-workflows-release/tree/HEAD/submissions/phosphor/skills/lda-forensics/lda-detect) · ★11)
- **lda-effect-size-biomarker-ranking**: Use when a genus-level ASV abundance table split into predefined classes (e.g., hypervariable region combinations) must be screened for differentially abundant taxa and ranked as biomarkers by LDA effect size. - **lda-enrich**: Pull external government data (live Senate LDA API, live House LDA search, usaspending.gov awards, FEC, FARA, Congress.gov, Federal Register) for an entity under investigation, cached with request-URL and timestamp provenance. - **lda-entities**: Resolve organizations across House and Senate lobbying records. Builds a key_aliases crosswalk (so cross-chamber joins don't flag phantom "filed in only one chamber" gaps) and an entity_names canonical map (so aggregations don't split one org across name-string variants). Run after lda-index and before any cross-entity comparison or aggregation. ([comp-journalism/aaijc-workflows-release](https://github.com/comp-journalism/aaijc-workflows-release/tree/HEAD/submissions/phosphor/skills/lda-forensics/lda-entities) · ★11)
- **lda-entity-resolver**: Build a cross-dataset entity table resolving lobbying registrants and clients across Senate LDA JSON and House LDA XML (which has no UUIDs and no standardized casing), using shared senateID/houseID plus normalized-name fuzzy matching. ([comp-journalism/aaijc-workflows-release](https://github.com/comp-journalism/aaijc-workflows-release/tree/HEAD/submissions/data-folks/skills/lda-entity-resolver) · ★11 · 2 copies)
- **lda-forensics**: Master orchestrator for investigating a Lobbying Disclosure Act (LDA) corpus. Sequences the component skills (lda-index, lda-entities, lda-profile, lda-detect, lda-enrich, lda-verify, lda-present) and routes by intent — a bottom-up anomaly sweep (restatements, cross-chamber gaps) or a top-down topic / "influence mapper" investigation (money vs. narrative vs. target on a policy topic) — ending with dossiers, an updated case file, and an HTML dossier. - **lda-index**: Build a zero-dependency SQLite index over a lobbying-disclosure corpus (Senate LDA JSON, House LDA XML, Congress Press JSONL) so investigation queries are SQL instead of file scanning. Indexes both chambers in full — filings, activities, lobbyists, government entities lobbied, foreign entities, Senate LD-203 contributions, House registrations, and a House topic full-text index.
